Final report

Findings: what blocks a full reproduction of Ryu et al. 2024 from its public materials

What ran. pinellolab/bean_manuscript @ 63dc7a8, all 35 notebooks, unmodified.

  • The environment is the authors' own environment.yml at exact pins, with the GPU-only packages dropped on this CPU host.
  • It has crispr-bean 0.2.9, the version the paper states, installed from its GitHub release tag because it is not on PyPI. The repository's environment file says 0.2.5.
  • The authors' Snakemake workflow and the raw reads from SRA PRJNA1042659 (220 FASTQ files, 12.4 GB, md5-verified) were used.
  • Result: 3 of 35 notebooks run without error. All remaining failures trace to the paper's materials, listed below, and none to a disagreement with the paper's results.

Materials that are missing or inconsistent

1. Raw reads are incomplete in the stated BioProject.

- LDLvar: 120 of the 168 FASTQ files the code expects are present; replicates 1–4 and their technical repeats (x.1) are absent. The deposit has a replicate 16 the code never uses.

- LDLRCDS: 70 of 118 are present; replicates 1–4 and x.1 are absent.

- CBE libraries (SpRY, CasNG): SRA has one run per replicate, while the code expects one file per sorting bin (top, high, bulk, low, bot).

- bean-count-samples requires every listed sample, so the read-mapping step cannot run as published.

2. The guide table does not match any published bean version. resources/gRNA_info/LDLRCDS_gRNA_bean.csv has a Reporter column, but bean-count 0.2.5 and 0.2.9 both require reporter.

3. The deposited processed object does not match the code. The Zenodo object bean_count_LDLRCDS_annotated_0.1_0.3.h5ad (record 10139794) carries allele tables for editing window 2–7. The code asks for window 0–19 (sig_allele_counts_spacer_0_19_A.G_translated_prop0.1_0.3), so bean-run and two notebooks stop there.

4. The code and environment file disagree with the paper. The code imports unpublished helper modules (anbe_tool, screen_simulation), and the environment file pins bean 0.2.5 where the paper states 0.2.9.

5. Other inputs are not public: supplementary Excel tables (resources/LDLvar/*.xlsx, 0223_LDLvar_Individual_FACSsummary.xlsx), and paths on the authors' machine (/data/pinello/PROJECTS/2021_08_ANBE/...).

Environment adaptations IGVF Agent made. None of these edit the authors' code; each is recorded in the run's report:

  • Dropped the GPU-only packages cudatoolkit, cudnn and pytorch-mutex.
  • gpu_to_cpu: the authors' --cuda code runs on the CPU.
  • mpl_missing_style: the unpublished matplotlib style jr falls back to the default style.
  • Installed pyro-ppl 1.8.6, which is missing from the declared environment, without upgrading the authors' torch 1.12.1.
  • Repaired Windows line endings in the shebangs of five bean 0.2.9 scripts.
  • Installed MOODS, pybedtools and dash-bio from conda-forge/bioconda.
